The modeling of an extensive ionospheric storm dataset over South America suggests accurate and reliable means to compute SBAS ionospheric corrections in the most challenging space weather environment using regional models that are more informed and responsive than local fitting performed by currently-operating SBAS.

</details>


### [76] [Operator Neural Jump ODEs: $L^2$-optimal prediction in function spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23110)
*Florian Krach,Oliver Löthgren,Josef Teichmann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将神经跳跃常微分方程（Neural Jump ODEs）推广到无限维函数空间，使NJ-ODE框架能够处理函数值随机过程（如收益率曲线、波动率曲面）的最优预测问题，并提出了新的逼近策略来证明收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NJ-ODE模型仅限于有限维随机过程，无法直接处理函数值问题（如收益率曲线、波动率曲面预测），只能通过离散化处理导致信息损失。本文旨在将NJ-ODE扩展到无限维输出过程。

Method: 借鉴神经算子（Neural Operator）方法的思想，将NJ-ODE扩展至无限维输出过程。底层过程X取值于L²(Ξ, ℝ^{d_X})而非ℝ^{d_X}，算子NJ-ODE通过生成条件期望的代表元来逼近最优预测。开发了新的逼近策略，在更宽松的假设下证明收敛性。

Result: 成功将NJ-ODE框架扩展到无限维函数空间，使模型能够处理函数值随机过程的最优预测。新的逼近策略不仅适用于无限维设置，也改进了先前有限维工作的收敛性证明，因为假设条件被显著弱化。

Conclusion: 本文通过神经算子方法实现了NJ-ODE从有限维到无限维的扩展，为函数值随机过程的在线学习预测提供了理论基础，同时新的逼近策略为收敛性分析提供了更通用的框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the extension of Neural Jump ODEs to infinite-dimensional function spaces. In particular, the underlying process $X$ now takes values in $L^2(Ξ, \mathbb{R}^{d_X})$ instead of $\mathbb{R}^{d_X}$ and the Operator NJ-ODE approximates the optimal predictor of this process by producing a representative of the conditional expectation. The NJ-ODE model is a framework for online learning the optimal prediction of continuous-time stochastic processes, given discrete, possibly irregular and incomplete past observations. In a series of works, this model has been extended to deal with generic path-dependent processes, with observation noise and dependent observations, with long-term predictions, and with input-output systems. However, throughout all of these works, the underlying processes were restricted to be finite-dimensional. In particular, function-valued problems, like yield curve or volatility surface predictions, could only be handled through discretization, which inherently leads to a loss of information. In this work, we build on ideas from Neural Operator methods that allow us to extend the NJ-ODE framework to an infinite-dimensional output process. To prove convergence of the NJ-ODE to the optimal prediction process, we develop a new approximation strategy that also generalizes previous works in the finite-dimensional setting by considerably weakening the assumptions.

</details>


### [77] [Adaptive Multi-Scale Forecasting and Gate-Localized Conformal Prediction for Multivariate Nonstationary Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23165)
*Ziling Ma,Junshu Jiang,Ángel López-Oriona,Ying Sun,Hernando Ombao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出ABF-T-GLCP框架，通过自适应预测状态表示实现非平稳多元时间序列的点预测与不确定性量化，结合门控机制与共形预测，在商品预测基准上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳多元时间序列的预测和不确定性量化具有挑战性，现有方法难以同时实现准确的点预测和自适应的预测区间校准。

Method: (1) 点预测模块：通过门控机制融合时间特定专家，利用稀疏预测转移跨序列改进预测；(2) 不确定性模块（GLCP）：利用门控状态和时间近邻性选择局部校准残差，将不确定性校准与预测模式耦合。

Result: 在大规模高频商品预测基准上，点预测精度一致提升，预测区间更窄且经验覆盖接近名义水平，框架可推广至金融以外的应用。

Conclusion: 共享表示机制使点预测和预测区间在时变动态下一致适应，同时保持共形预测的模型无关性，在温和稳定性条件下实现近似局部覆盖。

Abstract: We propose ABF-T-GLCP, a model-agnostic framework for forecasting and uncertainty quantification in nonstationary multivariate time series. The central idea is to learn an adaptive predictive state representation for point forecasting and reuse it for conformal calibration. The forecasting module combines horizon-specific temporal experts through a learned gate and refines predictions using sparse predictive transfer across related series. The uncertainty module, Gate-Localized Conformal Prediction (GLCP), uses the learned gate state, together with temporal recency, to select locally relevant calibration residuals, thereby coupling uncertainty calibration to the predictive regimes used by the forecasting model. This shared representation allows point forecasts and prediction intervals to adapt consistently under evolving temporal dynamics while retaining the model-agnostic nature of conformal prediction and yielding approximate local coverage under mild stability conditions. Experiments on a large-scale high-frequency commodity forecasting benchmark show consistent gains in point forecasting accuracy and substantially narrower prediction intervals with empirical coverage close to the nominal level. Additional results indicate that the framework extends beyond the motivating financial application.

</details>


### [78] [Beyond ICA: Identifiability by Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23182)
*Pengzhou Wu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了具有分段仿射解码器和高斯混合模型先验的深度生成模型在纯无监督设置下的可辨识性，通过引入三种代数对比原则打破对称性，并建立了从律可辨识性到后验可辨识性的层次化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无监督学习场景下，深度生成模型的可辨识性问题一直悬而未决。现有的可辨识性结果通常依赖连续性假设、解码器单射性或特定的学习算法，无法处理分段仿射解码器和非单射情形。作者旨在建立一套纯代数化的对称性破缺框架，突破这些限制。

Method: 提出了三种代数对比原则：(1) 域对比——平凡化混合对称群；(2) 机制对比——确保每个解码器分支被唯一的边界见证；(3) 交互对比——禁止潜变量成分与解码器分支之间的参数共谋。利用PWA映射的离散组合结构与潜变量GMM连续对称结构之间的相互作用，用代数对称条件替代连续性，将单射性从结构辨识中解耦，仅用于逐点求逆。

Result: 建立了可辨识性层次：律可辨识性（LID，潜变量分布至多差一个全局仿射映射）→ 映射可辨识性（MID，解码器至多差同一仿射映射）→ 后验可辨识性和逐点可辨识性。在对角分量协方差条件下，ICA形式的歧义性自然呈现。这是首个以代数对称性破缺为引擎的非线性可辨识性结果，首个允许不连续解码器，首个处理完全非单射解码器（每个观测对应多个潜变量编码）的工作。

Conclusion: 本文为深度生成模型的可辨识性提供了全新的代数化理论框架，打破了传统方法对连续性和单射性的依赖，拓展了可辨识性理论的适用范围。除了交互对比原则外，所有假设仅作用于数据生成过程而非学习方法。

Abstract: We prove the identifiability of deep generative models (DGMs) with piecewise-affine (PWA) decoders and Gaussian mixture model (GMM) priors, in a purely unsupervised setting. We introduce three algebraic contrast principles for symmetry breaking: domain contrast, which trivializes the mixture symmetry group; mechanism contrast, which ensures every decoder branch is witnessed by a unique boundary; and interaction contrast, which forbids parameter conspiracies between latent components and decoder branches. Together they exploit the interplay between the discrete combinatorics of the PWA map and the continuous symmetry structure of the latent GMM. Continuity is replaced by algebraic symmetry conditions; injectivity is decoupled from structural identification and required only for pointwise inversion. Our results form a hierarchy: from law identifiability (LID; latent distribution up to a global affine map) through map identifiability (MID; decoder up to the same map) to posterior and pointwise identifiability. The ICA-form ambiguity emerges under conditions on diagonal component covariances. Assumptions are only on the data-generating process, not on learning methods, except for the interaction contrast. To our knowledge this is the first to make algebraic symmetry-breaking the engine of nonlinear identifiability, the first to admit discontinuous decoders, and the first to handle fully non-injective decoders, where every observation admits multiple latent codes.

</details>


### [79] [FedSLIM: Privacy-Preserving Federated MDL-Based Descriptive Pattern Mining Across Data Silos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23236)
*Samar Samir Khalil,Noha S. Tawfik,Marco Spruit*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: FedSLIM是首个基于MDL（最小描述长度）的联邦描述性模式挖掘框架，通过两种变体在分布式数据库上协作优化紧凑模式模型，无需共享原始事务。实验表明其能保持高质量压缩结构，恢复全局信息模式，并揭示了分布式MDL挖掘中的局部-全局发现差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在预测建模上取得了显著成功，但联邦描述性分析（特别是模式挖掘）尚未被充分探索。现有联邦模式挖掘方法主要基于支持度，缺乏像最小描述长度（MDL）这样的原理性全局目标函数。

Method: 基于SLIM原则，提出了FedSLIM框架，包含两种互补变体以在不同部署假设下平衡隐私、通信和优化保真度；引入保真度和发现导向的评估指标来量化与集中式基线的一致性及全局信息模式的恢复能力。

Result: 在IID和非IID划分的多真实数据集上，两种变体均保持高质量压缩结构，所需搜索量比集中式基线低数个数量级；两种变体都能恢复所有独立局部模型均无法发现的全局信息模式。

Conclusion: 联邦MDL挖掘可作为隐私保护描述性分析的实用基础，应用于分布式数据孤岛。同时揭示了分布式MDL挖掘中局部-全局发现差距这一重要现象。

Abstract: Federated learning has achieved considerable success for predictive modelling, yet federated descriptive analytics remains largely unexplored. Existing federated pattern mining approaches are predominantly support-based and do not optimise a principled global objective such as Minimum Description Length (MDL). We introduce FedSLIM, the first federated MDL-based framework for descriptive pattern mining. Building on the SLIM principle, FedSLIM enables collaborative optimisation of compact pattern models across distributed databases without sharing raw transactions. We propose two complementary variants that balance privacy, communication, and optimisation fidelity under different deployment assumptions. To evaluate federated MDL mining, we introduce fidelity and discovery-oriented metrics that quantify agreement with a centralised baseline and assess recovery of globally informative patterns. Experiments on multiple real-world datasets under IID and non-IID partitioning show that both variants preserve high-quality compression structure while requiring orders of magnitude less search than the centralised baseline. We further reveal a local-global discovery gap in distributed MDL mining, where globally compressive patterns may be undiscoverable through isolated local optimisation. Both variants recover globally informative patterns absent from all standalone local models, demonstrating the benefits of federated optimisation beyond independent local mining. These results establish federated MDL mining as a practical foundation for privacy-preserving descriptive analytics across distributed data silos.

</details>


### [80] [Learning Asymptotics with Convergence-Rate Guarantees using Linear Least Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23287)
*Christos N. Efrem*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了渐近学习理论（ALT），一个结合优化与渐近分析的新研究领域。ALT利用优化理论统一计算渐近展开中的未知常数/参数。重点研究了滑动线性最小二乘（sLLSQ）和滑动Tikhonov线性最小二乘（sT-LLSQ）两种数值方法，证明了其收敛性和收敛速率保证，但也指出了局限性。在解析组合学中展示了应用，并通过数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有渐近分析方法在处理未知常数/参数时缺乏统一的优化框架，且传统方法（如比值法及其变体）存在局限性。作者希望建立一种将优化理论与渐近分析相结合的统一理论，以系统性地计算渐近展开中的未知参数，并克服现有方法的不足。

Method: 提出Asymptotics Learning Theory (ALT)框架，将渐近分析问题转化为优化问题；针对通用渐近形式，设计了滑动线性最小二乘（sLLSQ）和滑动Tikhonov线性最小二乘（sT-LLSQ）两种数值方法，并进行了严格的收敛性分析和收敛速率证明。

Result: 严格证明了sLLSQ和sT-LLSQ方法在满足充分条件时收敛到正确参数值，并给出了收敛速率保证；同时也揭示了两种方法的局限性，包括在某些情况下收敛缓慢甚至发散；在解析组合学中的应用验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: ALT为渐近分析中的参数计算提供了统一的优化理论框架，sLLSQ和sT-LLSQ是有效但并非万能的工具。该工作补充了比值法等现有方法，并为未来的研究方向（如更高效的算法设计、更广泛的渐近形式适用性等）奠定了基础。

Abstract: We introduce a new research area that is called Asymptotics Learning Theory (ALT) and combines optimization with asymptotic analysis. In particular, ALT provides a unified approach for computing unknown constants/parameters in proven asymptotic expansions using optimization theory. In this paper, we focus on a general asymptotic form which includes a broad class of asymptotics. Furthermore, we study two powerful numerical methods, namely, sliding Linear Least Squares (sLLSQ) and sliding Tikhonov Linear Least Squares (sT-LLSQ). For these techniques we rigorously prove asymptotic estimates that lead to sufficient conditions for convergence (to the correct values of unknown parameters) and convergence-rate guarantees. Despite their strengths, both methods have also limitations, e.g., slow convergence---or even, counterintuitively, divergence---in some cases. Moreover, we present fundamental applications in analytic combinatorics, a beautiful field of mathematics that deals with asymptotic enumeration of discrete structures using complex analysis. The proposed techniques complement existing approaches, such as the ratio method and its variants. Numerical examples also verify the theoretical results. Finally, we discuss interesting research directions in ALT.

</details>


### [81] [Context-Adaptive Inference: A Unified Statistical and Foundation-Model View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23304)
*Yue Yao,Caleb N. Ellington,Jingyun Jia,Baiheng Chen,Dong Liu,Rikhil Rao,Jiaqi Wang,Samuel Wales-McGrath,Yixin Yang,Zhiyuan Li,Eric P. Xing,Ben Lengerich*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了"上下文自适应推理"的统一视角，将三种传统上独立处理的方法（统计中的显式自适应、元学习中的快速任务自适应、大模型中的隐式自适应）形式化为共同目标：将上下文c映射到自适应参数θ(c)，再通过f(x; θ(c))进行预测。在特定条件下，证明显式参数自适应与隐式路由在数学上等价于核岭回归。提出了适应性效率、路由稳定性等评估指标及设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测系统需要根据具体情境自适应行为，但显式自适应（统计模型）、快速任务自适应（元学习）和隐式自适应（大模型）三种范式被独立研究，缺乏统一的理论框架来理解其内在联系和核心设计原则。

Method: 提出统一形式化框架：上下文c映射到自适应参数θ(c)，预测为f(x; θ(c))。在平方损失、线性预测头、固定特征条件下，通过数学推导证明显式参数自适应与隐式路由等价于上下文和输入的联合特征上的核岭回归。

Result: 建立了三种自适应范式之间的数学等价性桥梁；提出了适应性效率、路由稳定性和上下文特定鲁棒性等新的评估指标；识别了可辨识性、分布偏移鲁棒性和大规模高效自适应等开放问题。

Conclusion: 上下文自适应推理可被统一理解，设计原则应关注何时特化、如何约束特化以及如何审计部署中的自适应模型。未来的关键挑战包括可辨识性、分布偏移下的鲁棒性和大规模高效自适应。

Abstract: Modern predictive systems are expected to adapt their behavior to the specific situation they are facing. A clinical model should not treat every patient the same; a retrieval-augmented model should change its answer when given different evidence; a mixture-of-experts model should route different inputs to different experts. We call this capability context-adaptive inference: before predicting, the system uses information about the current context to specialize its parameters or computation for that instance.
  This article provides a unified view of context-adaptive inference across three traditions that are usually treated separately: (i) explicit adaptation in statistics (e.g. varying-coefficient models, local regression, hierarchical sharing), (ii) rapid task-specific adaptation in meta-learning and transfer, and (iii) implicit adaptation in large foundation models via prompting, retrieval, and expert routing. We formalize these approaches under a common objective: to map context $c$ to adapted parameters $θ(c)$, then to predict via $f(x; θ(c))$. Under squared loss, linear prediction heads, and fixed features, we prove that explicit parameter adaptation and implicit routing are mathematically equivalent to kernel ridge regression on joint features of inputs and context. Building on this bridge, we propose practical design principles and evaluation metrics including adaptation-efficiency, routing stability, and context-specific robustness to guide when to specialize, how to constrain that specialization, and how to audit context-adaptive models in deployment. Finally, we identify open problems in identifiability, robustness under distribution shift, and efficient large-scale adaptation, outlining design principles for methods that are scalable, reliable, and transparent in real-world settings.

</details>


### [82] [Logit-Coordinate Generative Models for Mixed Continuous-Categorical Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23348)
*Yuefei Shen,Xiaotong Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出logit坐标框架统一处理混合连续-分类数据，通过理论分析和实验验证了Logit Flow Matching和Logit Diffusion在类别不平衡场景下的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合连续-分类数据对连续生成模型构成表征难题：流匹配和高斯扩散作用于欧氏空间，而分类数据位于概率单纯形上且常存在严重不平衡，缺乏统一的理论框架和有效的处理方法。

Method: 提出logit坐标框架：将分类变量编码为平滑后的自然参数（logit变换），并与变换后的数值变量结合，形成Logit Flow Matching和Logit Diffusion的统一表述。引入混合分布差异度量，将分类边际误差与条件连续Wasserstein误差分离，并推导了向量场/漂移误差到解码后混合分布误差的稳定性界和非参数率。

Result: 仿真实验表明，scaled-logit坐标在严重稀有细胞不平衡场景下优于或匹配one-hot坐标。在四个真实数据基准、每个数据集10个划分上的实验显示：Logit FM在三个数据集上改进了主要分布指标，在Churn2上可比；Block-Conditional Logit FM持续改进flat模型；Logit Diffusion通常优于或匹配One-Hot Diffusion。

Conclusion: Logit坐标框架为混合连续-分类数据生成提供了有效的统一方法，尤其在类别不平衡场景下表现优异，理论分析和实验验证均支持其有效性。

Abstract: Mixed continuous--categorical data pose a representation problem for continuous generative models. Flow Matching and Gaussian diffusion operate in Euclidean spaces, whereas categorical laws lie on probability simplices and may be highly imbalanced. We study a logit-coordinate framework that encodes categorical variables as smoothed natural parameters and combines them with transformed numerical variables. This yields common formulations of Logit Flow Matching and Logit Diffusion. We introduce a mixed-distribution discrepancy separating categorical marginal error from conditional continuous Wasserstein error, and derive stability bounds and imbalance-aware nonparametric rates linking vector-field or drift error to decoded mixed-distribution error. Controlled simulations show that scaled-logit coordinates improve or match one-hot coordinates, especially under severe rare-cell imbalance. Across four real-data benchmarks and ten splits per dataset, Logit FM improves the primary distributional metrics on three datasets and is comparable on Churn2; Block-Conditional Logit FM consistently improves the flat model; and Logit Diffusion generally improves over or matches One-Hot Diffusion.

</details>


### [83] [Two-Timescale Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Resilient Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23434)
*Young Hyun Cho,Franz Stoll,Will Wei Sun,Guang Lin,Stephan Biller*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个双时间尺度的分层强化学习框架，用于联合优化长期和短期运营决策，在理论上证明了耦合双时间尺度学习的收敛性保证（平均差距为$O(T^{-1/2})$，在清晰利润损失场景下改进至$O(\log T/T)$），并在二手车案例研究中验证了该框架在需求-供给冲击下相比最强部分自适应基线提高平均利润9.2%，在长期冲击场景下提高11.8%，同时保持更稳定的利润轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球运营中频繁出现意外冲击，需要能够随市场和运营条件变化而自适应的决策规则。许多运营系统具有层级结构，其中长期和短期决策追求共同目标，但现有方法未能有效联合调整这些相互依赖的规则以增强韧性。

Method: 开发了一个双时间尺度分层强化学习框架，分别在各自的时间尺度上自适应长期和短期策略。由于策略相互依赖，框架同步其更新，并证明了耦合双时间尺度学习的收敛性保证。在二手车案例研究中，库存补货为长期决策，客户到达定价为短期决策。

Result: 理论结果表明，经过T个时期，策略与最优策略对的平均差距为$O(T^{-1/2})$，当糟糕决策产生更清晰的利润损失时改善至$O(\log T/T)$。在二手车案例中，相比最强部分自适应基线，联合需求-供给冲击下平均利润提高9.2%，长期冲击场景下提高11.8%，且利润轨迹更稳定。

Conclusion: 短期自适应足以应对常规季节性和单边中断，但在联合需求-供给冲击下，短期自适应单独不足，需要长期自适应为短期决策创造有利条件。联合自适应能在中断和恢复过程中产生更高且更稳定的利润。由于许多组织已采用分层规划，该框架无需改变现有决策结构即可增强运营韧性。

Abstract: Unexpected shocks recur in global operations, requiring decision rules that adapt as market and operating conditions change. Many operational systems also have hierarchical structures in which long-term and short-term decisions pursue a shared objective. We study how hierarchical reinforcement learning can strengthen resilience by adapting these interdependent rules jointly. We develop a two-timescale hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that adapts long-term and short-term policies at their respective time scales. Because the policies are interdependent, we synchronize their updates and prove, to our knowledge, the first convergence guarantees for coupled two-timescale learning. Over $T$ periods, our policies' average gap from an optimal policy pair is $O(T^{-1/2})$, improving to $O(\log T/T)$ when poor decisions produce clearer profit losses. In a used-car case study, inventory replenishment is the long-term decision and customer-arrival pricing the short-term decision. Relative to the strongest partially adaptive benchmark, the framework increases mean profit by $9.2\%$ under joint demand-supply shocks and by $11.8\%$ under a prolonged shock scenario, while maintaining a more stable profit trajectory over time. Short-term adaptation addresses routine seasonality and one-sided disruptions by responding immediately to changing conditions. Under joint demand-supply shocks, however, it is insufficient alone; long-term adaptation is also needed to create favorable conditions for short-term decisions. Joint adaptation thus yields higher and more stable profits through disruption and recovery. Because many organizations already use hierarchical planning, the framework strengthens operational resilience without altering existing decision structures.

</details>


### [84] [Learning switched non-linear dynamical systems from a single trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23502)
*Sunny G. W. Wang,Hemant Tyagi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究切换非线性动力系统的经验风险最小化问题，在稳定性假设和独立同分布切换条件下，推导了基于函数类度量熵的预测风险非渐近界，并为Hölder和线性函数类建立了依赖于有效样本量Tp_i的显式收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习算法缺乏对切换非线性动力系统从单条轨迹中学习的非渐近理论保证，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 在系统稳定性和K种模态独立同分布切换的假设下，利用经验风险最小化框架，基于函数类的度量熵推导预测风险的非渐近上界。

Result: 得到了Hölder函数类和线性函数类的显式收敛速率，该速率取决于有效样本量Tp_i（T为轨迹长度，p_i为模态i的出现概率）。数值实验支持理论结果。

Conclusion: 这是首个为从单条轨迹学习切换非线性动力系统提供非渐近理论保证的工作。

Abstract: We study empirical risk minimization for learning non-linear dynamical systems whose transition dynamics may switch over time. Under stability assumptions, and i.i.d switching over a set of $K$ modes, we derive non-asymptotic bounds on the prediction risk expressed in terms of the metric entropy of the underlying function class. We instantiate our general result for Hölder and linear function classes, obtaining explicit convergence rates that depend on the effective sample size $Tp_i$, where $T$ is the trajectory length and $p_i$ is the probability of observing mode $i$. Numerical simulations support our theoretical findings. To the best of our knowledge, these results are the first non-asymptotic guarantees for learning switched nonlinear dynamical systems from a single trajectory.

</details>


### [85] [Distributional Split Criteria for Random Forests: Extensions, Shrinkage, and the Robustness of Mean Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23721)
*Silas Koemen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了分布随机森林中多种分裂准则（各向同性/各向异性MMD、自适应频率选择、切片Wasserstein距离及后验核均值收缩），发现：各向同性MMD已接近最优；单变量回归中基于均值的CART分裂仍是稳健默认；多变量响应场景下分布分裂优势显著。分布分裂仅在存在可估计的非位置结构时才有帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将分类回归树（CART）基于均值的分裂准则扩展为基于完整条件响应分布的比较，以处理非位置结构（如异方差、多模态等）带来的复杂数据模式，但各类分布分裂准则的相对优劣尚不明确，需要系统评估。

Method: 在同一诚实森林框架内实现了五种分布分裂准则（各向同性随机傅里叶特征MMD、各向异性对角带宽MMD、自适应逐分裂频率选择MMD、非核切片Wasserstein距离、后验核均值收缩），通过配对种子比较法在合成分位数机制、真实单变量基准、加州住房子样本曲线、多变量合成与真实响应数据上进行系统评估。

Result: (1) 各向同性MMD已接近最优，各向异性、自适应频率、切片Wasserstein及后验收缩未系统性提升性能；(2) 标量表格回归中，基于均值的CART分裂仍是稳健默认，在多数场景胜出；(3) 多变量响应是分布分裂的明确优势场景，尤其在纯依赖copula数据上，能量评分能区分各准则而边际CRPS不能。

Conclusion: 分布分裂仅在非位置结构既存在又可估计时才有帮助，否则会稀释分裂选择中关于均值的信号。所有准则、诚实森林及配对比较工具已开源在drforest库中。

Abstract: Distributional random forests replace mean-based CART splitting with criteria that compare the full conditional response distribution in candidate children. We implement and systematically study a family of such criteria inside a single honest-forest implementation: isotropic random-Fourier-feature maximum mean discrepancy (MMD), an anisotropic diagonal-bandwidth variant, an adaptive per-split frequency-selection variant, and a non-kernel sliced-Wasserstein criterion, together with post-hoc kernel-mean shrinkage of the forest weights. Using paired-seed comparisons across synthetic quantile mechanisms, real univariate benchmarks, a California-housing subsample curve, and multivariate synthetic and real responses, we characterize where each extension pays. Three findings recur. First, among distributional criteria ordinary isotropic MMD is already close to best in class: the anisotropic, adaptive-frequency, and sliced-Wasserstein extensions, and post-hoc shrinkage, do not systematically improve on it. Second, on scalar tabular regression mean-based CART splitting remains the robust default and wins many cells. Third, multivariate responses are the regime where distributional splitting clearly earns its keep, most sharply on a pure-dependence copula where the energy score separates the criteria even though marginal CRPS does not. The evidence supports a simple allocation story: distributional splitting helps only when non-location structure is both present and estimable; otherwise it dilutes split-selection power away from the mean. All criteria, the honest forest, and the paired-comparison harness are implemented in the open-source \texttt{drforest} library, whose Rust-backed split search makes broad criterion sweeps inexpensive.

</details>


### [86] [On Non-Stationary Dynamic Pricing: Adaptivity and Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24115)
*Feiyu Jiang,Zifeng Zhao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了非平稳环境下的上下文动态定价问题，提出了一种基于多尺度变点检测的自适应算法，实现了最优遗憾界（O(√(s_T d T) ∧ {V_T^{1/3} d^{1/3} T^{2/3} + √(dT)})），并证明了该算法对变化类型自适应的"两全其美"最优性，同时给出了匹配的极小化下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态定价研究大多假设需求模型在时间上保持不变，但实际市场环境常存在结构性变化。非平稳环境下的上下文动态定价问题尚未被充分研究，且已有的非平稳bandit方法无法直接应用于上下文动态定价场景。

Method: 提出基于多尺度变点检测的算法，自适应地检测需求模型参数的变化；使用广义线性模型（GLM）刻画需求；定义了新的"设计调整变化预算"（design-adjusted variation budget）概念来刻画参数变化程度；算法无需预知分段数s_T或变化预算V_T。

Result: 实现了O(√(s_T d T) ∧ {V_T^{1/3} d^{1/3} T^{2/3} + √(dT)})的遗憾界，并给出了匹配的极小化下界（对数因子内最优），通过大量数值实验验证了算法的效率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该算法是首个对变化类型自适应的动态定价算法，实现了"两全其美"的最优率，填补了文献中长期存在的空白；同时证明了非平稳bandit方法无法直接应用于此问题。

Abstract: We study the contextual dynamic pricing problem under non-stationarity, where a firm sells products to $T$ sequentially arriving consumers that behave according to an unknown demand model that can change over time. The demand model is assumed to be a generalized linear model (GLM), allowing for a feature vector in $\mathbb{R}^d$ that encodes products and consumer information. To achieve optimal revenue (i.e., least regret), the firm needs to learn and exploit the unknown GLMs while monitoring for potential changes. We propose a multiscale change-point detection based algorithm that achieves a regret of order $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{s_TdT}\wedge\{V_T^{1/3}d^{1/3}T^{2/3}+\sqrt{dT}\})$, where $s_T$ is the number of piecewise stationary segments and $V_T$ is a newly defined notion of design-adjusted variation budget of model parameters. Our algorithm is adaptive and does not require knowing $s_T$ or $V_T$. Moreover, to our knowledge, this is the first dynamic pricing algorithm that is adaptive to the nature of changes and achieves the best-of-both-worlds rate, thus closing a long-standing gap in the literature. We remark that, due to the varying contexts, existing works in the adaptive non-stationary bandit literature cannot be applied to achieve optimality for contextual dynamic pricing. The regret is further accompanied with a newly constructed minimax lower bound, confirming the optimality of our algorithm (up to logarithmic factors). Extensive numerical experiments are conducted to illustrate the efficiency and robustness of the proposed algorithm in non-stationary dynamic pricing.

</details>


### [87] [Minimax Lower Bounds of Kernel Discrepancy Estimation: MMD, HSIC, KSD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24235)
*Jose Cribeiro-Ramallo,Florian Kalinke,Zoltán Szabó*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了在一般拓扑空间和温和核假设下，最大均值差异（MMD）、希尔伯特-施密特独立性准则（HSIC）和核斯坦差异（KSD）等核差异估计的极小极小下界为n^{-1/2}，从而解决了这些核差异最优估计的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知在有限维欧氏空间和有界核的严格假设下，核差异的估计率n^{-1/2}是极小极优的，但在更一般的拓扑空间和无界核场景下，其最优性尚不清楚。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 通过理论分析，在一般拓扑空间和核的温和假设下，推导了MMD、HSIC和KSD等核差异估计的极小极小下界，并同时证明了均值嵌入和中心化互协方差算子估计的相同速率。

Result: 证明了在一般拓扑空间和温和核假设下，MMD、HSIC和KSD估计的极小极小下界均为n^{-1/2}，均值嵌入和中心化互协方差算子估计也达到相同速率。

Conclusion: 这些结果解决了这些流行核差异估计的最优性问题，表明n^{-1/2}的收敛率在更广泛的设定下仍然是极小极优的。

Abstract: Over the past 20 years, kernel discrepancies have been leveraged as a highly powerful tool for quantifying the disagreement of distributions, with numerous successful applications in two-sample, goodness-of-fit, and independence testing, among others. Their fastest estimators are known to converge at a parametric rate---$n^{-1/2}$---under mild conditions. While this rate is known to be minimax optimal on $\mathbb R^d$ under strict assumptions with bounded kernels, little is known about its optimality beyond the finite-dimensional Euclidean setting with unbounded kernels. In this work, we prove that the minimax lower bound of estimation of the most popular kernel discrepancies (maximum mean discrepancy, Hilbert-Schmidt independence criterion and kernel Stein discrepancy; MMD, HSIC, KSD) is $n^{-1/2}$ on general topological spaces, and under mild assumptions on the kernel; the same rates are shown (as corollaries) to hold for the estimation of the mean embedding and the centered cross-covariance operator. Our results settle the question of optimal estimation of these kernel discrepancies.

</details>


### [88] [proxymate: Diagnosis and Adjustment of Proxy Estimates for Reliable Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24401)
*Alexandra N. M. Darmon,Deeksha Sinha,Steve Wilkins-Reeves,Caner Gocmen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了proxymate框架和开源Python包，用于代理结果的验证和调整。该框架包含四个层级（代表性、单元、估计、领域），提供诊断检查和针对性调整策略，已在Meta多个业务场景中应用，评估和修正了数百万代理-主要单元的比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理结果（如短期行为信号、模型预测或替代终点）常被用于替代成熟期长、罕见或难以直接测量的主要结果，但基于代理的估计可能存在系统性偏差，导致置信区间校准不当，因此需要系统化的验证和调整框架。

Method: 提出了proxymate框架，包含四个层级：代表性层级（人群有效性）、单元层级（测量质量）、估计层级（决策有效性）和领域层级（跨领域可迁移性）。每个层级提供诊断检查和针对性的调整策略，将特定失败映射到适当的修正方法。

Result: 在Meta被多个用例采纳，涵盖实验、流行率估计和监控场景，评估和修正了数百万个代理-主要单元比较，促进了多个工作流的启动，包括支持数千个实验的快速决策。

Conclusion: proxymate框架有效解决了代理结果系统性偏差的问题，通过四层级诊断和调整策略，在不同业务场景中验证了其模块化和实用性，为大规模代理评估提供了系统化解决方案。

Abstract: Proxy outcomes (such as short-term behavioral signals, model predictions, or surrogate endpoints) are frequently used in place of primary outcomes that are too slow to mature, rare, or challenging to measure directly. But valid inference on a proxy does not guarantee valid inference on the primary estimate as proxy-based estimates can be systematically biased in ways that are difficult to predict, leading to improperly calibrated confidence intervals.
  We present proxymate, a framework and open-source Python package for proxy validation and adjustment. proxymate organizes into four levels: The Representativity Level (population validity), the Unit Level (measurement quality), the Estimate Level (decision validity), and the Domain Level (cross-domain transportability). Within each level, proxymate provides diagnostic checks, and targeted adjustment strategies that map specific failures to appropriate corrections.
  At Meta, proxymate has been adopted by many different use cases, spanning experimentation, prevalence estimation, and monitoring use cases, all facing different proxy challenges (limited human review time, long maturation window of outcomes, low detectability) and showcasing the modularity of the framework. Across all products, proxymate assessed and corrected millions of proxy, primary unit comparisons. It has facilitated launches across multiple work streams including enabling quick decision making on thousands of experiments.

</details>


### [89] [Frequency-Based Reservoir computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24420)
*Arthur S Powanwe*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受大脑振荡动力学和时标层次启发的频率基储备池（频域储备池），将储备池解释为独立振荡单元的集成，每个单元处理输入的部分频率内容。利用非线性振荡器受复杂周期输入驱动的理论，发现频率基单元选择性地放大和存储特定输入频率用于预测。该方法在性能上达到或超过传统随机储备池，且可优化以改进短时预测，并能预测复杂时空动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有储备池计算依赖随机连接的网络和大量超参数优化，缺乏精确解释其工作机制和优化方法的理论框架。

Method: 提出频率基储备池：将储备池建模为独立振荡单元的集成，每个单元处理输入信号的不同频率成分；借用受迫非线性振荡器理论分析单元行为。

Result: 频率基储备池性能达到或超过等效随机储备池；可优化以改进短时预测（随机储备池不具备此特性）；能预测复杂的时空动力学。

Conclusion: 储备池计算可以借助大脑特性和受迫非线性振荡器物理学的理论洞察来设计，从而实现可解释且可优化的计算框架。

Abstract: Reservoir computing has emerged as an efficient machine learning framework for predicting time series generated by dynamical systems. In contrast to other machine and deep learning approaches, a reservoir computing trains only the output layer via linear regression, leaving the reservoir (recurrent layer) untrained. This simplification makes reservoir computers easier to train and more amenable to experimentation. However, because current reservoirs consist of networks of randomly connected nodes and require the optimization of numerous hyperparameters, a framework that precisely explains how reservoir computing operates and how it can be optimized remains missing. Here, we propose a frequency-based reservoir inspired by the brain's oscillatory dynamics and its hierarchy of timescales. The frequency-based reservoir can be interpreted as an ensemble of independent oscillatory units, each processing a portion of the input's frequency content.
  This allows us to understand the reservoir's internal behavior by modeling it as a single unit driven by an external input. Borrowing from the theory of a nonlinear oscillator forced by complex periodic inputs, we found that units of the frequency-based reservoir selectively amplify and store specific input frequencies, which are then used for prediction. The frequency-based reservoir performs as well as or better than equivalent random reservoirs. Furthermore, the frequency-based approach can be optimized to improve short-term prediction, a property that random reservoirs lack. Finally, we show that the frequency-based reservoir can also predict complex spatiotemporal dynamics. Our results show that reservoir computing can be designed using brain properties and theoretical insights borrowed from the physics of forced nonlinear oscillators.

</details>


### [90] [A Model for Imbalanced Label Aggregation: A Focus on Minority-Class Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24622)
*Gabriel Singer,Samuel Gruffaz,Olivier Vo Van,Nicolas Vayatis,Argyris Kalogeratos*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对不平衡众包数据中的类别依赖标注者准确率问题，提出了一种结合项目难度和类别依赖标注者能力的生成聚合模型。该模型在33个真实众包数据集上表现优异，尤其在少数类召回率方面显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有众包聚合模型要么捕捉类别依赖错误但忽略项目难度，要么建模项目难度但无法捕捉类别依赖错误，无法同时处理这两方面问题。而在实际检测系统中，最具操作重要性的标签往往也是最稀有的，因此需要一种能同时建模项目难度和类别依赖标注者能力的模型。

Method: 提出一种生成聚合模型，结合项目难度与类别依赖的标注者能力，允许标注者能力和项目难度在不同类别间变化。重新审视了类别不平衡设置下的Condorcet陪审团定理，并证明多数投票在渐进意义上能保留底层类别比例。

Result: 在33个真实众包数据集（涵盖图像和文本的多分类任务，以及大规模标注数据集和大规模项目数据集）上进行评估，该模型在少数类召回率上持续达到最高，同时在平衡准确率上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 该模型在不平衡众包数据中尤其适用于以稀有标签恢复为主要目标的场景，填补了现有模型无法同时处理项目难度和类别依赖错误的研究空白。

Abstract: We study imbalanced crowdsourcing with a focus on class-dependent annotator accuracy, a setting that, to the best of our knowledge, remains relatively underexplored despite its importance in real-world inspection systems where the labels of greatest operational importance are also the rarest ones. In this setting, annotators may be reliable on both classes, unreliable on both classes, majority-class specialists, or minority-class specialists. Existing models only partially address this problem: they either capture class-dependent errors but ignore item difficulty, or they model item difficulty without capturing class-dependent errors. To fill this gap for imbalanced datasets in crowdsourcing, we introduce a generative aggregation model combining item difficulty with class-dependent annotator competence. The model allows both annotator abilities and item difficulties to vary across classes. We then revisit Condorcet's Jury Theorem in the class-imbalanced setting. We also show that majority voting asymptotically preserves the underlying class proportion. We evaluate our model on $33$ real-world crowdsourcing datasets, covering multiclass tasks such as images and text, as well as two large-scale regimes: large-scale annotation datasets, with many annotations per item, and large-scale item datasets, with a large number of annotated instances. Across these diverse settings, our model consistently achieves the highest minority recall while remaining competitive in balanced accuracy, making it particularly relevant when rare-label recovery is the primary objective.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [91] [Concept-based Visual Counterfactual Explanations with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22544)
*Yassine Oueslati,Daniil Kirilenko,Martin Gjoreski,Marc Langheinrich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出C-VCE框架，通过将分类器直接嵌入扩散模型的概念瓶颈层，生成基于可解释语义概念的反事实解释，无需外部噪声鲁棒分类器，在保持图像真实性的同时实现更可控、更小扰动的反事实编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的反事实解释方法依赖外部分类器，但分类器在噪声图像上不可靠，导致系统脆弱、难以部署。本文希望构建一个无需外部分类器、基于可解释概念引导的鲁棒反事实解释框架。

Method: C-VCE将分类器直接构建在生成模型内部的概念瓶颈层上，允许用户在采样过程中开关语义概念；添加概率正则化项平衡"改变预测"与"保持原图接近"；使用基于梯度的掩码限制修改区域，保持特征相关性。

Result: 在CelebA等基准上，C-VCE在翻转率上与基线方法相当或更优，同时生成的反事实在视觉上更接近输入图像、失真更小。

Conclusion: 暴露并控制内部概念层是增强生成模型可理解性和安全性的有效途径，C-VCE为安全关键领域提供了实用的"假设"图像生成工具，无需依赖额外的噪声鲁棒分类器。

Abstract: Visual counterfactual explanations aim to answer "what minimal change to this image would flip the model's prediction?", and are increasingly important as vision models are deployed in safety-critical domains (e.g., medicine). Existing diffusion-based methods can produce realistic edits, but they rely on external classifiers that must work reliably on noisy images, which makes them fragile and hard to deploy for robust explanations. We introduce C-VCE, a new diffusion framework that builds the classifier directly into the generative model via a concept bottleneck layer, so that counterfactuals are guided by human-interpretable features (concepts) instead of a separate noise robust classifier that works with pixel-level edits. Our model lets users to toggle on/off semantic concepts during sampling, then minimally adjusts relevant image regions, while preserving the rest of the image, respecting feature correlations. To keep edits small and controlled, we add a simple probabilistic regularizer that balances "change the prediction" against "stay close to the original", plus a gradient-based mask that confines modifications to the most relevant regions. On benchmarks such as CelebA, C-VCE matches or improves flip rates while producing counterfactuals that are visually closer to the input and less distorted than baselines that depend on separate noisy-image classifiers. These properties make C-VCE a practical tool for vision systems where users need concrete "what-if" images without having to trust an additional, noise-robust classifier. More broadly, our results suggest that exposing and controlling an internal concept layer is a promising way to make powerful generative models easier to understand and safer to use.

</details>


### [92] [SeT-Diff: Towards Semantic Foundation Models for HPC Telemetry and Time-Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22548)
*Giovanni B. Esposito,Francesco Antici,Daniele Cesarini,Andrea Bartolini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SeT-Diff，首个面向计算节点遥测时间序列的基础模型，基于扩散过程并利用传感器语义描述进行条件生成，在重构、插补、预测和虚拟传感等任务上表现出色，MAE低至0.047，且具备零样本排列稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HPC遥测的机器学习方法通常依赖固定位置的匿名传感器变量的静态子集，仅适用于单一任务，当目标任务变化或传感器指标变化时模型即失效，缺乏灵活性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出SeT-Diff，一种基于扩散生成模型的基础模型，通过将生成过程条件化于每个传感器的语义描述，将系统动力学与数据集结构解耦，从而实现对不同传感器配置和任务的泛化。

Result: 在真实超算数据集上，重构任务MAE为0.0470；零样本排列稳定性良好，传感器打乱后精度几乎无下降；单个预训练模型即可完成数据插补、预测和虚拟传感，热推断MAE为0.033。

Conclusion: SeT-Diff作为一种数据驱动的数字孪生方案，有效解决了HPC系统中模型的灵活性和可迁移性问题，为计算节点遥测提供了通用的基础模型框架。

Abstract: Data centers and their compute nodes require accurate and flexible digital twins capable of modeling the complex interplay of workloads, environmental parameters, and physical metrics. Current machine learning approaches for HPC and its telemetry typically rely on a static subset of anonymous, fixed-position sensor variables tailored to single tasks. Consequently, these models become obsolete when target tasks change or sensor metrics vary. We propose SeT-Diff, the first foundational model for compute node telemetry and time-series. Unlike rigid architectures, our diffusion-based approach conditions the generative process on each sensor's semantic description, decoupling the system dynamics from the structure of the dataset. Experiments on a real-world supercomputer dataset demonstrate a Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 0.0470 on reconstruction tasks. SeT-Diff exhibits zero-shot permutation stability, maintaining accuracy with negligible degradation even when sensors are shuffled. A single pre-trained model effectively performs data imputation, forecasting, and virtual sensing - achieving a 0.033 MAE in thermal inference - making SeT-Diff an effective data-driven digital twin for HPC systems.

</details>


### [93] [QFoldAgent: An Autonomous Quantum Optimization Multi-Agent System for Protein Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22549)
*Winson Chen,Yuqi Zhang,Sixu Chen,Nuo Xu,Qiang Guan,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QFoldAgent，一个用于5残基四面体晶格折叠的闭环多智能体框架。该框架通过设计智能体提出序列条件惩罚项，VQE量子-经典管线在噪声下优化哈密顿量，反馈智能体利用能量景观诊断和MolProbity验证信号迭代改进惩罚项。在QDockBank基准上，中位RMSD从3.64Å降至3.20Å；在未见序列上，结构有效性从87.5%提升至98.7%，恢复87%初始无效案例。结果表明迭代智能体控制能系统性改善5残基量子场景下的优化行为并减少失败案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子-经典蛋白质结构预测高度依赖哈密顿量惩罚权重，但现有晶格流程通常手工固定这些系数，且仅评估极短片段。为解决这一手工调参的局限性和评估范围不足的问题，本文提出迭代多智能体框架自动优化惩罚项。

Method: 采用闭合环路多智能体框架：1）设计智能体提出序列条件惩罚系数；2）VQE量子-经典管线在Qiskit Aer噪声下优化哈密顿量；3）反馈智能体基于能量景观诊断和MolProbity验证信号迭代改进惩罚项。仅在评估阶段使用RMSD等真实指标，不暴露给智能体。

Result: QDockBank基准上中位RMSD从3.64Å降至3.20Å，最难目标增益最大。未见序列上结构有效性从87.5%提升至98.7%，恢复87%初始无效案例；最强控制器使87%序列的cycle-3能量改善，同时保持96%的Ramachandran偏好几何结构。

Conclusion: 迭代智能体控制可系统性改善5残基量子设置下的优化行为并减少失败案例，展示了闭环多智能体方法在量子蛋白质结构预测中的潜力。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical protein structure prediction depends strongly on Hamiltonian penalty weights, yet existing lattice-based workflows typically fix these coefficients by hand and evaluate only very short fragments in simulation. We present QFoldAgent, a closed-loop multi-agent framework for 5-residue tetrahedral-lattice folding in which a design agent proposes sequence-conditioned penalties, a VQE-based quantum-classical pipeline optimizes the resulting Hamiltonian under Qiskit Aer noise, and a feedback agent uses energy-landscape diagnostics and MolProbity validation signals to refine penalties across cycles. Ground-truth metrics such as RMSD are never exposed to the agents and are used only for evaluation. We study the framework on two complementary datasets: 55 QDockBank-derived fragments with known structures and 100 coverage-optimized unseen sequences. On the QDockBank benchmark, QFoldAgent reduces median RMSD from 3.64 Å to 3.20 Å, with the largest gains on the hardest targets. On unseen sequences, the closed loop raises structural validity from 87.5% to 98.7%, recovers 87% of initially invalid cases, and the strongest controller improves cycle-3 energy on 87% of sequences while maintaining 96% Ramachandran-favored geometry. These results show that iterative agent control can systematically improve optimization behavior and reduce failure cases in a 5-residue quantum setting.

</details>


### [94] [Same Question, Different Answers: Evaluating LLM Reliability Beyond Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22554)
*Kazem Faghih,Yize Cheng,Shoumik Saha,Mobina Pournemat,Armin Gerami,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM在不同但等价的问法下答案不一致，单次提示的正确性不能反映模型可靠性，简单自释义策略可部分恢复潜在知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试仅衡量LLM的平均准确性，但忽视了模型在语义等价的变体问法下是否保持一致的响应，作者旨在揭示这一可靠性差距。

Method: 在4个基准（事实问答与数学推理）上对13个模型进行语义保持的释义变换，比较同一问题的不同问法下模型输出的稳定性，并设计了一种简单的自释义策略来提升推理性能。

Result: 总体准确率变化不大，但实例级行为不稳定，答案翻转率超过23%；单次回答正确不能保证可靠性；模型至少对某一释义能给出正确答案，表明知识存在但检索不一致。

Conclusion: 标准准确率指标掩盖了实质性的不稳定性，评估等价输入下的一致性才是衡量LLM可靠性的更清晰的指标。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often achieve strong accuracy on benchmarks, yet it remains unclear how reliably they apply this knowledge when the same question is phrased in different but equivalent ways. In this work, we study how model answers change under meaning-preserving paraphrases across factual question answering and mathematical reasoning tasks. Across four benchmarks and 13 models, we find that model outputs frequently depend on the exact wording of the prompt. While overall accuracy typically changes only modestly across paraphrases, instance-level behavior is far less stable: for many questions, models alternate between correct and incorrect answers depending on phrasing, with mismatch rates reaching more than 23%. Conditioning on questions that are answered correctly in their original form reveals even larger failures measured by answer flip rates, showing that single-prompt correctness is often a poor indicator of reliability. At the same time, we find that models often produce a correct answer for at least one paraphrase of a question, suggesting that the underlying knowledge is present but inconsistently retrieved. Building on this observation, we show that a simple self-paraphrasing strategy can partially recover this latent knowledge and improve performance at inference time. Together, these findings suggest that standard accuracy metrics can mask substantial instability, and that evaluating consistency across equivalent inputs provides a clearer picture of LLM reliability.

</details>


### [95] [DeepLens Diagnosis Agent: Agentic Workflow Design Lets a Small Reasoning Model Compete with Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22555)
*Mahmood Bayeshi,Veysel Kocaman,Muhammed Ali Naqvi,Yigit Gul,David Talby*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepLens Diagnosis Agent，一个五阶段诊断管线，以JSL Medical Small 7B v2小模型和RAG为核心，通过结构化流程约束在DiagnosisArena基准上达到60.14%的top-1诊断准确率，比无管线版本提升36个百分点，成本仅0.0072美元/例，优于Claude Sonnet 4.5和Gemini 3.1 Pro等大模型。管线产生的结构化中间产物支持可审计、可追溯的诊断推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前沿LLM虽然是强大的通才，但在医疗诊断中单次提示往往产生脆弱的推理结果；且小模型知识回忆能力强（医疗基准88.2%）但诊断推理能力差（无管线仅23.99%），说明诊断推理需要超越知识回忆的流程约束。因此需要设计一种可约束、可审计、成本低廉的诊断管线。

Method: 五阶段管线：1) 结构化临床提取（结构化提取事实）；2) 纪律性检索（RAG）；3) 约束性候选生成；4) 显式证据三角验证；5) 可审计的最终决策。核心模型为JSL Medical Small 7B v2小模型，结合流程约束与模型能力。

Result: 在915例DiagnosisArena上top-1准确率60.14%，为小/中型模型中最高；无管线版本仅23.99%，提升36个百分点；成本0.0072美元/例（24K tokens），比Claude Sonnet 4.5便宜35-45%，且准确率高出9.70pp和9.17pp；延迟24秒。

Conclusion: 流程约束（harnessing）可以超越参数量或API成本的影响，即使小模型也能通过结构化管线超越大模型；该管线产生结构化中间产物，支持错误定位、可追溯、可审计，适用于高风险医疗场景。

Abstract: Medical diagnosis is a multi-stage process: extract facts, consult knowledge, generate a differential analysis, and select the best diagnosis with explanations. Frontier LLMs are strong generalists, but single-shot prompting often yields brittle diagnostic reasoning. We present the DeepLens Diagnosis Agent, a five-stage harnessing pipeline (combining model capabilities with disciplined process constraints) centered on a small medical reasoning model (JSL Medical Small 7B v2) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The pipeline enforces structured clinical extraction, disciplined retrieval, constrained candidate generation, explicit evidence triangulation, and an auditable final decision. On the 915-case DiagnosisArena benchmark, the agent achieved 60.14% top-1 diagnostic accuracy, the highest among small and medium-sized models. The same model without the agent workflow achieved 23.99%, a +36-point gain from workflow design alone, despite 88.2% on standard medical benchmarks, showing that diagnostic reasoning under uncertainty requires more than knowledge recall. The agent costs USD 0.0072 per case (24K tokens on A100) with 24-second latency, 35-45% cheaper than Claude Sonnet 4.5 (USD 0.0110) and Gemini 3.1 Pro (USD 0.0128) while outperforming them by +9.70pp and +9.17pp. Harnessing can also correct frontier model failures; workflow constraints can outweigh parameter count or API cost.
  Beyond aggregate accuracy, the pipeline produces structured intermediate artifacts that make each stage inspectable and support error localization. These properties support high-stakes settings where traceability, reproducibility, and auditable evidence matter alongside benchmark performance.

</details>


### [96] [MIITA: Memory-Induced Inference-Time Adaptation for Continual Learning with Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22556)
*Dong Li,Yanchi Liu,Xujiang Zhao,Wei Cheng,Zhengzhang Chen,Xintao Wu,Zhong Chen,Chen Zhao,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIITA是一种面向资源受限场景下小语言模型（SLM）的监督持续学习框架，通过存储压缩的修正方向原型与语义锚点，在推理时以门控临时隐藏状态适配实现非破坏性的旧知识复用，无需主干更新、提示扩展或测试时反向传播，有效缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法对SLM不适用：直接更新参数空间有限导致灾难性遗忘；基于记忆的方法依赖大语言模型（LLM）的充足存储和强上下文推理能力，而SLM缺乏这些条件。因此需要一种适合SLM资源受限部署的持续学习方案。

Method: 1) 将监督经验存储为压缩的修正方向原型与语义锚点；2) 推理时基于语义和不确定性线索检索；3) 通过门控临时隐藏状态适配（gated temporary hidden-state adaptation）应用检索到的方向，实现无需主干更新、提示扩展或测试时反向传播的旧知识复用。

Result: 在多种监督持续学习设置下的广泛实验表明，MIITA在固定记忆预算下持续提升了最终性能并有效缓解了遗忘问题。

Conclusion: MIITA是一种有效解决SLM在资源受限场景下持续学习问题的框架，通过内存驱动的推理时适配机制实现了非破坏性的知识复用，其设计有局部理论分析支持（一阶损失减少、不确定性引导检索、方向覆盖度）。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) is essential for small language models (SLMs) to adapt to evolving real-world needs in resource-constrained deployments. However, directly updating their limited parameter space causes catastrophic forgetting. While memory-based methods naturally address this by decoupling knowledge retention from parameters, existing approaches designed for large language models (LLMs) rely on abundant storage and strong in-context reasoning that SLMs lack. To address these challenges, we propose MIITA, a Memory-Induced Inference-Time Adaptation framework for supervised CL under constrained storage. MIITA stores supervised experiences as compact correction-direction prototypes with semantic anchors, and retrieves them at inference time using semantic and uncertainty-based cues. The retrieved directions are applied through gated temporary hidden-state adaptation, enabling non-destructive reuse of past supervision without backbone updates, prompt extensions, or test-time backpropagation. A local theoretical analysis links this design to first-order loss reduction, uncertainty-guided retrieval, and directional coverage for retaining old-stage knowledge. Extensive experiments across diverse supervised CL settings show that MIITA consistently improves final performance and mitigates forgetting under fixed memory budgets.

</details>


### [97] [Codifying the Judge: Scalable Evaluation via Program Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22561)
*Tzu-Heng Huang,Shengqi Qiu,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PAJAMA系统，通过将LLM评估逻辑蒸馏为程序化评判委员会，实现了低成本、高透明度、低延迟的自动评估方法，在多项任务上媲美13B量级LLM评判性能，并在奖励信号蒸馏上以极低API成本超越基于专有LLM的奖励模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为评判者虽已成为自动评估的标准范式，但存在高成本、显著延迟以及决策不透明等局限性，这些缺陷削弱了其可扩展性和可靠性。本文旨在寻找一种简单高效的替代方案来解决这些问题。

Method: 提出程序蒸馏（program distillation）方法：将LLM的决策逻辑蒸馏为一组程序化评判委员会，直接对候选对象进行评分。基于此构建PAJAMA系统，该系统合成程序作为评判者，聚合其决策形成联合裁决，并引入后备机制将低置信度案例选择性升级到LLM处理。

Result: 在5个数据集和4个模型系列上，程序化评判者可以匹配13B规模LLM评判的性能。PAJAMA系统在准确率和吞吐量上均有提升，推进了帕累托前沿。在RewardBench上，通过程序化评判的裁决蒸馏出的奖励模型，以两个数量级更低的API成本超越了基于专有LLM标签训练的奖励模型。

Conclusion: 程序化评判者是LLM评判的高效、透明且经济的替代方案，不仅适用于自动评估，在奖励信号生成方面也具有显著优势，为大规模自动化评估提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge has become the standard for automated evaluation, but it suffers from high cost, significant latency, and opaque decisions -- limitations that undermine its scalability and reliability. We address these with a simple, efficient alternative: program distillation. Instead of prompting an LLM at the evaluation time, we distill its decision logic into a committee of programs that score candidates directly. These programmatic judges offer transparency, are easily inspected or edited, and eliminate per-sample API costs. Building on this notion, we introduce PAJAMA, a system that synthesizes programs as judges, aggregates their decisions into a joint verdict, and incorporates a fallback mechanism to selectively escalate low-confidence cases to an LLM. Across five datasets and four model families, we show that programmatic judges can match the performance of a 13B-size LLM judge. When using program outputs as routing signals, PAJAMA improves both accuracy and throughput and advances the Pareto frontier. Beyond evaluation, programmatic judges produce cheap and effective reward signals: on RewardBench, a reward model distilled from programs' verdicts outperforms one trained on a proprietary LLM's labels at two orders of magnitude lower API cost.

</details>


### [98] [SF-AMS: Strategic Forgetting for Structured Memory in LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22562)
*Ning Yang,Siqi Li,Miaoxin Shen,Yuan Zhou,Meng Zhang,Tong Li,Haijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SF-AMS提出了一种基于效用驱动的选择性遗忘框架，通过建模记忆单元的长时重要性来维护紧凑高效的智能体记忆，在多项长上下文推理任务中显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体中冗余和无关信息会降低多步推理性能，现有静态检索和启发式衰减方法无法有效维护高价值记忆。

Method: 提出效用驱动的记忆生存机制，结合使用冗余和时序信号更新记忆重要性；设计复合重要性评分融合语义和实体级信号；形成层次化记忆结构，优先保留稳定的实体一致信息。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-s上全面超越LightMem、MemO、A-Mem等基线。Qwen2.5-7B下多跳推理F1提升+9.65，GPT-4o-mini下时序推理提升+6.91 F1，开放域任务提升+6.53 F1。

Conclusion: 将记忆重要性建模为动态效用信号是提升长上下文推理可靠性的关键，SF-AMS展现了跨模型骨干的强泛化能力。

Abstract: Managing long-context dependencies remains a primary bottleneck in LLM agents, as redundant and irrelevant information can degrade multi-step reasoning. Strategic Forgetting for Agent Memory Systems (SF-AMS) is proposed as a framework for maintaining compact high-utility memory by modeling the long-term importance of memory units. SF-AMS replaces static retrieval and heuristic decay with a utility-driven survival mechanism that updates memory importance from usage redundancy and temporal signals, inducing a hierarchical memory structure that prioritizes stable entity-consistent information while filtering noise. On top of this, Composite Importance Scoring integrates semantic and entity level signals to improve retrieval robustness. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-s show consistent gains over strong state of the art baselines including LightMem MemO and A-Mem. The largest improvement appears in multi-hop reasoning under Qwen2.5-7B where SF-AMS achieves plus 9.65 F1 over the strongest baseline followed by temporal reasoning under GPT-4o-mini plus 6.91 F1 and open-domain tasks plus 6.53 F1 demonstrating strong cross backbone generalization. These results show that modeling memory importance as a dynamic utility signal is critical for reliable long-context reasoning.

</details>


### [99] [Synthetic Scenario Generation for Evaluation of Industry 4.0 Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22563)
*Sagar Chethan Kumar,Rohith Kanathur,Dhaval Patel,Kaoutar El Maghraoui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ScenarioGeneratorAgent，一个基于IEC标准的高效合成场景生成流水线，将AssetOpsBench扩展到智能电网变压器领域。通过两级缓存、并行生成、线程池、批量LLM调用和早期拒绝过滤等优化，在50个场景生成中实现8倍加速，同时保持场景质量（质量评分74.2 vs 73.8）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业智能体基准测试（如AssetOpsBench）依赖人工编写场景，覆盖资产类别有限，缺乏可扩展的高质量场景生成方法。

Method: 提出ScenarioGeneratorAgent流水线，包含：证据基础的资产画像构建、覆盖感知的场景预算分配、混合验证-修复循环（保障模式有效性、工具可达性、物理合理性、标准对齐、去重），以及两级缓存、并行焦点组生成、线程池卸载、批量LLM调用、早期拒绝过滤等优化策略。

Result: 在智能电网变压器场景生成任务中，优化后的流水线在50个场景生成上实现8倍端到端加速，复合质量评分74.2±1.9，与未优化基线的73.8±3.0相当。

Conclusion: 基于标准的合成场景生成方法能高效扩展工业智能体基准测试，且不牺牲场景质量。

Abstract: Industrial agent benchmarks require realistic evaluation scenarios that integrate telemetry, failure modes, maintenance records, and domain standards. However, existing benchmarks such as AssetOpsBench rely on manually authored scenarios and cover a limited set of asset classes. We extend AssetOpsBench with a Smart Grid Transformer asset class and four IEC-grounded diagnostic tools for health-index prediction, dissolved-gas analysis, winding-temperature assessment, and load-profile assessment. We further introduce ScenarioGeneratorAgent, a pipeline for synthetic industrial-agent scenario generation. The pipeline constructs evidence-grounded asset profiles, allocates coverage-aware scenario budgets across operational domains, and generates candidates through a hybrid validation-and-repair loop that enforces schema validity, tool reachability, physical plausibility, standards alignment, and deduplication. To improve scalability, we apply two-level caching, parallel focus-group generation, thread-pool offloading, batched LLM calls, and early rejection filtering. On Smart Grid Transformer scenario generation, these optimizations reduce end-to-end runtime by $8\times$ for 50 scenarios while preserving quality, achieving a composite quality score of $74.2 \pm 1.9$ compared with $73.8 \pm 3.0$ for the unoptimized baseline. These results show that standards-grounded synthetic scenario generation can efficiently expand industrial-agent benchmarks without sacrificing scenario quality.

</details>


### [100] [Loss-Aware Feature-Map Pruning in Convolutional Neural Networks Using Multi-Armed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22564)
*Salem Ameen,Sunil Vadera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多臂老虎机的损失感知特征图剪枝框架，将每个候选特征图视为一个臂，通过临时掩码评估损失变化并生成安全移除奖励，在固定预算后永久移除top-k特征图及其关联的滤波器、偏置和下层输入通道核。实验表明UCB1和Thompson Sampling在多个数据集上保持接近未剪枝模型的精度，显著优于贪心和基于幅度的剪枝方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积神经网络常包含冗余特征图，增加了存储和推理成本。现有剪枝方法多为非结构化剪枝（移除孤立标量权重），而结构化剪枝（移除完整输出通道及其滤波器）能更有效地加速网络，但需要一种损失感知的评估机制来确定哪些特征图是冗余的。

Method: 将每个候选特征图视为一个臂，在每次游戏中临时掩码一个特征图并在采样小批量数据上评估，观察损失变化并转化为安全移除奖励，然后恢复该图。在固定预算后，根据学习到的分数对候选图排序，永久移除top-k特征图及其对应的滤波器、偏置和下一层输入通道核。评估了UCB1和Thompson Sampling两种策略。

Result: 在LeNet/MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、SVHN、CUB-200-2011和Oxford Flowers 102等数据集上，UCB1和Thompson Sampling均能保持接近未剪枝模型的精度，同时显著减少特征图数量和卷积计算量。Friedman和Nemenyi检验显示UCB1获得最高平均秩，Thompson Sampling次之，两者均显著优于贪心和基于幅度的剪枝方法，且与原始未剪枝模型统计上无显著差异。

Conclusion: 基于多臂老虎机的损失感知特征图剪枝是一种有效的结构化剪枝框架，能够在保持模型精度的同时显著降低存储和推理成本。UCB1和Thompson Sampling在该任务上表现优异，其中UCB1略优。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks often contain redundant feature maps that increase storage and inference cost. This paper presents a loss-aware feature-map pruning framework using multi-armed bandits. Feature-map pruning is structured because it removes complete convolutional output channels and their producing filters rather than isolated scalar weights. Each candidate feature map is treated as an arm. At each play time, one map is temporarily masked and evaluated on a sampled mini-batch; the map is then restored and the observed loss change is converted into a safe-removal reward. After a fixed play budget, candidate maps are ranked by learned scores and the top-k maps are permanently removed with their filters, biases and corresponding next-layer input-channel kernels. The study evaluates UCB1 and Thompson Sampling, compares them with direct/oracle-style evaluation on LeNet/MNIST, and extends the evaluation to MNIST, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, SVHN, CUB-200-2011 and Oxford Flowers 102. Results show that UCB1 and Thompson Sampling preserve accuracy close to unpruned models while removing feature maps and reducing convolutional computation. Friedman and Nemenyi tests show that UCB1 obtains the highest mean rank, followed by Thompson Sampling; both significantly outperform greedy and magnitude-based pruning while remaining statistically comparable to the original unpruned model.

</details>


### [101] [DSTFView: Multi-View Cloud-Edge Workload Forecasting with Dual-Input Spatio-Temporal-Frequency Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22565)
*Qingzhong Li,Hui Ma,Yajun Zhang,Qingchang Ma,Zhou Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DSTFView，一种面向云边协同环境的双输入时空频多视角负载预测框架，通过联合建模邻近与周期依赖、提取时空频域特征、设计自适应融合机制，在CPU和TP数据集上优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘端AI推理的广泛部署要求边缘平台支持延迟敏感、高并发、高可靠的应用，但现有方法难以在云边协同环境中平衡多维特征建模与预测效率。

Method: 提出DSTFView框架，采用双输入结构联合建模邻近依赖和周期依赖，分别从空间域、时间域和频率域提取依赖关系，并设计自适应融合机制动态调整各视角的贡献以捕捉突变。

Result: 在CPU和TP数据集上的实验结果表明，DSTFView在多个预测跨度和评估指标上 consistently 优于代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: DSTFView通过多视角融合与自适应机制有效提升了云边协同环境下负载预测的准确性和鲁棒性，为边缘AI推理的可靠性保障提供了新方案。

Abstract: With the widespread deployment of edge-side AI inference, edge platforms are increasingly required to support latency-sensitive, highly concurrent, and reliability-critical applications. However, existing methods often struggle to balance multidimensional feature modeling and forecasting efficiency in collaborative cloud-edge environments. To address this issue, we propose DSTFView, a dual-input spatio-temporal-frequency multi-view workload forecasting framework for collaborative cloud-edge environments. It jointly models closeness and period dependencies and extracts spatial, temporal, and frequency-domain dependencies. Besides, it designs an adaptive fusion mechanism and adjusts the contribution of each view to capture abrupt changes. Experimental results on the CPU and TP datasets demonstrate that DSTFView consistently outperforms representative baselines across multiple forecasting horizons and evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [102] [MedLoCoMo: A Long-Context Multi-Session Medical Dialogue Benchmark for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22566)
*Zeyu Zhang,Ziqing Wang,Kaize Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedLoCoMo是一个医学长上下文记忆基准，用于评估LLM在多入院医疗对话中进行患者特定临床推理的能力，包含100个患者时间线（平均1669.8轮对话、29.7次就诊、74,512.2 tokens），实验发现跨入院推理比单次证据定位更困难，即使模型拥有长上下文窗口或使用外部记忆/检索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学QA基准主要测试短上下文知识或单文档推理，缺乏对LLM在纵向患者历史中进行使用、连接和拒答能力的评估。

Method: 基于去标识化的MIMIC-IV和MIMIC-IV-Note数据，构建入院级临床包、合成医患对话、生成证据关联的QA项（包括单入院、跨入院和对抗性不可回答三种设置）。

Result: 跨入院推理一致性地比局部证据使用更困难，且长上下文窗口或外部记忆/检索方法均未能消除这一差距。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在处理纵向患者历史的多入院临床推理方面仍存在显著不足，MedLoCoMo可作为评估和改进这一能力的重要基准。

Abstract: MedLoCoMo is a Medical Long-Context Memory benchmark for patient-specific clinical reasoning over multi-admission medical dialogue. Existing medical QA benchmarks largely test short context knowledge or single document grounding, leaving open whether LLMs can use, connect, and abstain over longitudinal patient histories. We build MedLoCoMo from deidentified MIMIC-IV and MIMIC-IV-Note records by constructing admission-level clinical packets, synthesizing grounded doctor-patient conversations, and generating evidence linked QA items over single-admission, cross-admission, and adversarial unanswerable settings. The benchmark contains 100 patient timelines averaging 1,669.8 turns, 29.7 sessions, and 74,512.2 tokens per conversation. Across the evaluated baselines, cross-admission reasoning is consistently harder than localized evidence use, even when models have long context windows or use external memory or retrieval methods. The code and MedLoCoMo benchmark release is available at https://github.com/leozzy13/MedLoCoMo for use and reproducibility.

</details>


### [103] [Keyword Matters: Unveiling the Energy Sensitivity of On-Device LLM Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22568)
*Ruiyi Tao,Xiaolong Tu,Haoxin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了提示词设计对设备端大语言模型能耗的影响，发现通过调整提示词的动词选择和指令结构可以显著降低能量消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在移动和嵌入式设备上部署时面临高能耗瓶颈，而现有的模型压缩和运行时加速研究未充分探索提示词设计对能效的影响。

Method: 在智能手机上通过真实功率测量，量化分析提示词的语言特征（特别是祈使动词和指令结构）对解码长度和总能耗的影响。

Result: 不同动词和任务之间存在一致的能耗差异，提示词设计可以作为轻量级的能效优化手段。

Conclusion: 提示词工程是一种有效的轻量级能效优化方法，通过优化提示词措辞即可在不改变模型本身的情况下降低设备端推理能耗。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed on mobile and embedded devices to improve privacy and reduce network latency. Yet on-device inference faces a fundamental constraint: high energy consumption on battery-powered, resource-limited hardware. While model compression and runtime acceleration have been widely studied, the effect of \emph{prompt design} on energy efficiency remains underexplored. This paper presents an empirical study of the relationship between prompt wording and energy consumption for on-device LLMs. Using real power measurements collected on a smartphone, we quantify how linguistic features, particularly imperative keywords and instruction structure, affect decoding length and total energy. Our results show consistent energy differences across verbs and tasks, indicating that prompt engineering is a lightweight lever for improving energy efficiency.

</details>


### [104] [Execution-Grounded Security Testing for Coding Agents in Software Engineering Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22569)
*Yifei Ge,Weisong Sun,Jinkun Xiao,Yuchen Chen,Yebo Feng,Peizhuo Lv,Xia Feng,Chunrong Fang,Zhihong Zhao,Zhenyu Chen,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种执行层红队测试框架，通过将危险操作嵌入常规软件工程任务（如单元测试、回归测试），利用可观察的沙箱证据（工具调用、运行时痕迹、文件系统差异）来探测编码代理的安全边界。实验表明，在多个代理框架上，该方法显著提高了不安全执行率（代码载体73.61%，文本载体53.93%），揭示了编码代理在系统运维中仍然面临伪装任务的安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理在系统运维中的工具使用可直接修改项目工件、执行环境和底层系统，其不安全行为可能持久化并滥用权限。现有安全测试主要关注代理输出内容，而忽视了其实际对执行环境造成的影响，因此需要一种关注执行层安全边界的红队测试方法。

Method: 提出执行层红队测试框架：（1）将目标危险操作嵌入常规软件工程工作负载（单元测试、回归测试、崩溃复现与验证）；（2）利用可观察沙箱证据（工具调用、运行时痕迹、文件系统差异）探测执行层安全边界；（3）采用执行预言机机制，在初始探测被拒绝或失败时引导细化和重试。

Result: 在多个代理框架和模型骨干上，红队工作负载重写显著提高了已验证的不安全执行率：代码载体达到73.61%，文本载体达到53.93%。

Conclusion: 编码代理在系统运维中仍然不安全：一旦危险意图被隐藏在看似合理的工程任务中，代理可能被诱导对周边系统执行危险操作。编码代理在系统运维中仍需要更强的安全测试和安全防护措施。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly integrated into system operations, where their tool use can directly modify project artifacts, execution environments, and the underlying system. For example, if a coding agent inserts a hook into a system startup or configuration script, that change can persist after the interaction, be triggered later, and abuse delegated user or system privileges to modify the system. This makes security testing a system problem: the key question is not only what the agent says, but what it actually does to the surrounding environment. We present an execution-grounded red-team testing framework for probing this execution-layer security boundary using observable sandbox evidence, including tool invocations, runtime traces, and file-system diffs. Our framework embeds target unsafe operations into routine software engineering workloads, including unit testing, regression testing, crash reproduction, and validation, and uses an execution oracle to guide refinement when an initial probe is rejected or fails. Across multiple agent frameworks and model backbones, our red-team workload reformulation substantially increases verified unsafe execution, reaching 73.61% on code carriers and 53.93% on text carriers. These results show that coding agents in system operations remain insecure under task disguise: once risky intent is hidden inside plausible engineering tasks, the agent can be induced to carry out unsafe actions on the surrounding system. More broadly, coding agents in system operations still demand stronger security testing and safeguards.

</details>


### [105] [Reference Feature Atlases for Mechanistic Auditing of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22570)
*Rui Wu,Tong Che*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出参考特征图谱（Reference Feature Atlas）：在参考模型集上训练稀疏特征库，新模型仅需拟合线性解码器即可复用，通过图谱通道和残差通道分别提供跨模型稳定坐标和显式审计信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型审计需要重新学习和解释每个新模型的内部特征，缺乏跨模型复用的高效工具。

Method: 在5个7-9B指令微调模型上训练留一法参考特征图谱，对Mistral和Qwen目标模型仅通过线性解码器适配；通过图谱通道（已解释的参考特征）和残差通道（图谱无法重构的新特征）进行审计。

Result: 残差通道可在运行时精确控制隐藏目标，匹配对照不受影响；在Mistral上检测到植入目标为最高排名的潜在特征，而per-target SAE和pairwise crosscoder基线失败；在Qwen-2.5上发现面板相对政治框架聚类，干预后仅改变审计框架指标。

Conclusion: 参考特征图谱实现了高效、可迁移的模型审计，残差通道能有效捕获非参考特征并作为显式审计信号，优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Auditing a new language model usually means relearning and reinterpreting its internal features from scratch. We propose a reference feature atlas: a sparse feature library trained once on a reference panel and reused for new targets, which attach by fitting only a linear decoder. This yields two complementary views. The atlas channel reads the target on already interpreted panel features, providing a stable coordinate system across models. The residual channel learns features only from what the atlas fails to reconstruct, making "outside the reference panel" an explicit audit signal.
  We train leave-one-out atlases over five 7-9B instruction-tuned models and audit held-out Mistral and Qwen targets. On three controlled LoRA hidden objectives injected into both targets, the residual channel makes the planted mechanism perfectly controllable at runtime while matched controls stay unaffected and recovers the planted objective as the top-ranked latent across both targets; on Mistral, where the per-target SAE and pairwise crosscoder baselines are retrained for a head-to-head benchmark, both baselines fail to do so. On Qwen-2.5, the same channel additionally reveals a panel-relative political-framing cluster; steering it shifts the audited framing metrics while out-of-domain controls remain unchanged.

</details>


### [106] [SCAIR: Schema-Conditioned Agentic Iterative Reasoning for Enterprise Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22571)
*Prateek Chaturvedi,Yuqicheng Zhu,Hongkuan Zhou,Dongzhuoran Zhou,Yunjie He,Steffen Staab,Fei Du,Jie Tang,Evgeny Kharlamov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SCAIR框架，通过注入模式条件结构先验和强制模式感知遍历，在无需训练的情况下显著提升了企业知识图谱上的检索增强生成性能，证明了将领域结构约束融入推理过程的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于智能体的KG-RAG方法在公开基准上表现良好，但无法泛化到真实企业知识图谱（密集、模式驱动、操作受限），缺乏对领域结构约束的显式建模。

Method: 提出SCAIR（Schema-Conditioned Agentic Iterative Reasoning），一种无需训练的框架，通过注入模式条件结构先验并在多跳推理中强制执行模式感知遍历，将结构化规划与受控迭代推理相结合。

Result: 在基于真实CMDB构建的企业级基准上，SCAIR显著优于现有KG-RAG方法。

Conclusion: 可靠的企业图谱推理不能依赖通用智能体设计，必须显式融入目标领域的结构和操作约束；通过将智能体设计与业务逻辑对齐，可在无需昂贵模型重训练的情况下取得显著性能提升。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (KG-RAG) enables natural language interaction with structured enterprise knowledge, yet existing agentic approaches that perform well on public benchmarks often fail to generalize to real-world enterprise Knowledge Graphs (KGs), which are dense, schema-driven, and operationally constrained. To address these limitations, we propose SCAIR (Schema-Conditioned Agentic Iterative Reasoning), a training-free framework that integrates structured planning with controlled iterative reasoning by injecting schema-conditioned structural priors and enforcing schema-aware traversal during multi-hop reasoning. Experiments on an enterprise-oriented benchmark constructed from a real-world Configuration Management DataBase (CMDB) demonstrate that SCAIR substantially improves performance over existing KG-RAG methods. Crucially, our study highlights that reliable enterprise graph reasoning cannot rely on generic agentic designs; instead, it must explicitly incorporate the target domain's structural and operational constraints into the reasoning process. We demonstrate that by aligning agent design with business logic, substantial performance gains can be achieved without the need for costly model retraining.

</details>


### [107] [Schema-Aware Localisation (SAL): Live Schema Grounding and Hallucination Validation for Oracle NL2SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22572)
*Sanjay Mishra,Divya Chukkapalli,Ganesh R. Naik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Schema-Aware Localisation (SAL)，一种无需模型重训练的轻量级Oracle NL2SQL中间层。通过查询实时数据字典构建schema映射、选择相关表子集注入提示词，并利用Hallucination Index (Hidx)进行别名-列引用验证与自动重写。在500个TPC-H问题上，SAL将执行成功率从2.2%（无schema）提升至62.6%（无需手动schema管理），执行失败率从97.6%降至2.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在真实Oracle企业数据库上生成SQL时，常因列名/别名的幻觉和方言语法缺失导致执行失败（ORA-00904错误），核心原因是模型缺乏对数据库真实schema的了解。

Method: 1) 查询Oracle USER_TAB_COLUMNS构建实时schema映射；2) 根据问题选择相关表子集（多表查询时回退至完整schema）注入LLM提示词；3) 通过Hallucination Index (Hidx)验证每个alias.column引用，自动重写可预测的前缀错误，否则触发带逐项修正的结构化重试。

Result: 在500个TPC-H问题+Oracle ADB 23c+GPT-4o-mini上：无schema grounding仅2.2% EGT；手动静态schema提示达62.0%；SAL达62.6% EGT（简单96%、中等95%、复杂40.7%），执行失败率从97.6%降至2.6%。

Conclusion: SAL通过轻量级实时schema感知和自动验证重写机制，无需模型重训练即可显著提升Oracle NL2SQL的可靠性，有效解决了企业级数据库中的schema幻觉问题。

Abstract: Large language models can generate fluent SQL from natural language, but on real enterprise Oracle databases they frequently fail at execution time: columns and aliases are hallucinated and dialect-specific syntax is missed, leading to ORA-00904 invalid-identifier errors. In this setting, failures are primarily due to missing schema grounding: the model cannot know which tables and columns actually exist. This paper introduces Schema-Aware Localisation (SAL), a lightweight middleware layer for Oracle NL2SQL that requires no model retraining. SAL queries Oracle's USER_TAB_COLUMNS catalog to build a live schema map, selects a relevant table subset for each question (falling back to the full schema for multi-table queries), and injects this ground-truth context into the LLM prompt. Generated SQL is then checked by the Hallucination Index (Hidx), which validates every alias.column reference against the live catalog, automatically rewrites predictable prefix errors, and otherwise triggers a structured retry with itemised corrections. We evaluate SAL on 500 TPC-H natural language questions executed against a live Oracle Autonomous Database 23c instance using GPT-4o-mini. Without any schema grounding, execution-grounded truth (EGT; executes and matches the reference result set) is 2.2% (12/500). A hand-written static schema hint brings EGT to 62.0%. SAL, with no manual schema curation, achieves 62.6% EGT (96% simple, 95% medium, 40.7% complex) while reducing execution failures from 97.6% to 2.6%.

</details>


### [108] [PhononBench-MP40: a spectrum-resolved benchmark dataset for phonon stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22573)
*Wen-Kao Li,Ze-Feng Gao,Zhong-Yi Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PhononBench-MP40数据集，包含46,899个来自Materials Project的声子谱计算记录（16,683稳定/30,216不稳定），并公开了YAML格式的声子谱、稳定性标签及计算失败记录，为声子稳定性分类和阈值研究提供可审计的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚频声子模式是计算材料筛选中的实际瓶颈，因为看似合理的结构在特定工作流下可能存在局部动力学不稳定性。现有缺乏可审计的基准数据集来系统研究工作流定义的声子稳定性分类问题。

Method: 从Materials Project的47,969个MP40工作流任务出发，经过计算得到46,899条完整记录，包含稳定性标签和本地phonopy YAML谱。将1,067条弛豫失败记录单独报告。数据集以YAML格式的本地声子谱为核心，稳定性标签、最低采样频率和阈值依赖的重新标注均从该谱导出。

Result: 构建了包含16,683条稳定记录和30,216条完整声子计算不稳定记录的基准数据集，共计46,899条。提供了Science Data Bank开放访问和GitHub辅助工具。

Conclusion: PhononBench-MP40为工作流定义的稳定性分类、最低频率分析、阈值研究和失败感知分类提供了可审计的参考基准，同时明确了参考工作流、数据模式和解释边界。

Abstract: Imaginary phonon modes remain a practical bottleneck in computational materials screening because otherwise plausible structures can be locally dynamically unstable under a chosen workflow. Here we present PhononBench-MP40, a spectrum-resolved benchmark dataset of Materials Project-derived crystals for workflow-defined phonon stability. The dataset starts from 47,969 MP40 workflow tasks and provides 46,899 completed records with paired stability labels and local phonopy YAML spectra, including 16,683 Stable records and 30,216 completed-phonon unstable records. A further 1,067 relaxation failures are reported separately rather than merged into the completed phonon denominator. The release centers on the local YAML spectrum: the stability label, the lowest sampled frequency and any threshold-dependent relabeling are derived from that spectrum. The dataset is openly available through Science Data Bank at https://doi.org/10.57760/sciencedb.38735. A companion GitHub repository provides the calculation code and lightweight access utilities. PhononBench-MP40 provides an auditable reference for workflow-defined stability classification, minimum-frequency analysis, threshold studies and failure-aware triage, while keeping the reference workflow, data schema and interpretation boundaries explicit.

</details>


### [109] [Too much evidence, too little time: From text to actionable recommendations through multi-objective evidence reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22574)
*Adela Bara,Simona-Vasilica Oprea*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SCEPTER框架，将临床病例描述转化为循证推荐，通过PubMed检索、语义排序、LLM提取、证据加权、矛盾检测和Pareto多目标优化，将576篇论文压缩为3条最终推荐（压缩比192:1），同时保持高证据多样性（熵=0.901）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策需要综合大量科学文献，但PubMed检索常返回数百篇文献，无法在时间限制下人工审阅，亟需自动化框架辅助循证决策。

Method: SCEPTER框架包含：PubMed检索、PubMedBERT语义排序、LLM基于文本的声明提取、证据级别加权、矛盾检测、共识分析、多目标Pareto声明选择，以及用于交互式探索的Paper Q&A模块。

Result: 在150个病例上，平均搜索空间从576篇降至53篇保留论文、7个Pareto最优声明和3条最终推荐，压缩比192:1；证据多样性熵值为0.901；消融实验表明Pareto选择优于传统排序方法。

Conclusion: SCEPTER将多目标推理、矛盾分析和交互式文献探究整合为统一的临床决策支持流水线，有效压缩文献空间的同时保持证据多样性与推荐效用。

Abstract: Evidence-based clinical decision making requires specialists to identify, evaluate and synthesize relevant scientific literature. However, PubMed searches for complex clinical cases often return hundreds of publications that cannot be reviewed manually under time constraints. This study proposes SCEPTER (Single-Case Evidence-driven PubMed-To-rEcommendation Reasoner), a framework for transforming clinical case descriptions into evidence-based recommendations. SCEPTER combines PubMed retrieval, PubMedBERT semantic ranking, large language model (LLM)-based claim extraction, evidence-level weighting, contradiction detection, consensus analysis and multi-objective Pareto claim selection. The framework generates structured evidence syntheses and grounded actionable recommendations. A Paper Q&A module further enables interactive exploration of selected publications. The proposed framework introduces multi-objective reasoning model that integrates literature support, contradiction analysis and interactive literature interrogation into a unified clinical decision-support pipeline. Evaluation on 150 case studies demonstrated that the framework reduced an average search space of 576 papers to 53 retained papers, 7 Pareto-optimal claims and 3 final recommendations, corresponding to an overall compression ratio of 192:1. Despite this reduction, the retained evidence maintained high diversity (entropy=0.901). The ablation study showed that Pareto-based selection increased evidence diversity and recommendation utility compared with conventional ranking approaches.

</details>


### [110] [Temporal Context Reinstatement Drives Episodic-Like Order Memory in Long-Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22575)
*Mathis Pink,Vy Ai Vo,Qinyuan Wu,Jianing Mu,Javier Turek,Uri Hasson,Kenneth A. Norman,Sebastian Michelmann,Alexander Huth,Mariya Toneva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了长上下文大语言模型（LLMs）是否以及如何捕捉人类情景记忆的核心行为特征。通过使用基于长篇小说全文记忆的人类行为数据集，发现模型表现出与人类相同的距离效应。运用长上下文机制可解释性分析揭示，模型依赖于一个一维时间编码，该编码在检索时通过单个时间重播注意力头实现。这支持了时间上下文重建作为LLMs中情景式时序记忆的重要机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类情景记忆支持对长时间尺度经历的检索，但其计算机制仍存在争议。长期记忆实验在人类中机制可及性有限，而长上下文LLMs可能为揭示这类检索的计算机制提供新途径。

Method: 使用基于长篇小说全文记忆的人类行为数据集，在时序记忆任务上比较LLMs与人类行为表现；应用长上下文机制可解释性分析，探究模型解决该任务的内在机制。

Result: LLMs表现出与人类相同的特征性距离效应；模型性能依赖于一维时间编码，该编码在检索时通过单个时间重播注意力头（time-reinstatement attention head）进行重建。

Conclusion: 时间上下文重建是LLMs中情景式时序记忆的重要机制，为理解人工和生物系统中长时情景记忆的时间性方面提供了新见解。

Abstract: Human episodic memory supports the retrieval of experiences that unfold over extended timescales, yet the computational mechanisms underlying this ability remain debated due to the limited mechanistic accessibility in long-term memory experiments in humans. Long-context LLMs may offer promising ways to reveal plausible computational mechanisms that drive this type of retrieval. Here, we investigate whether and how LLMs capture the core behavioral signatures of episodic memory via a temporal order memory task. Using a new dataset of human behavior based on memory of a full-length novel, we show that models exhibit the same characteristic distance effect observed in humans on this task. We next apply long-context mechanistic interpretability analyses to uncover how models solve this task, and find that model performance relies on a one-dimensional temporal code that is reinstated during retrieval by a single time-reinstatement attention head. These findings support temporal context reinstatement as an important mechanism for episodic-like temporal-order memory in LLMs, offering new insights into how temporal aspects of long-term episodic memory may be instantiated in both artificial and biological systems.

</details>


### [111] [cMoLLM at Scale: Horizontal Scaling Laws for Mixture-of-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22577)
*Xin Yang,Yemin Wang,Mingda Liu,Letian Li,Shuaishuai Cao,Zhengxiao He,Ryan Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出cMoLLM，将MoE混合层重新定义为可变核动态卷积，通过端到端流路由实现更高效、更稳定的LLM扩展，在困惑度和下游任务上优于ParaScale和AltUp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dense Transformers中每个token激活所有参数，导致计算和容量耦合，训练推理成本随模型规模线性增长，成为万亿参数模型的瓶颈。现有方法（ParaScale、AltUp）存在开销大、路由同质化、梯度坍塌、适应性有限等问题。

Method: 将MoE混合层重新表述为可变核动态卷积，每个专家对应一个1×1卷积核，路由实现输入条件的核聚合。基于此等价性，提出cMoLLM：通过完全可微的动态卷积对端到端流进行路由的卷积门控混合LLM。

Result: 在GPT-2风格模型上（FineWeb训练），cMoLLM在匹配计算条件下提升了语言建模困惑度、GLUE和SQuAD准确率；相比ParaScale和AltUp基线，具有更好的流利用率、更稳定的优化和更有利的扩展性。

Conclusion: 将MoE混合视为动态卷积的视角有效，cMoLLM通过可微门控机制实现了更优的容量扩展，解决了前人方法的关键缺陷。

Abstract: Scaling large language models (LLMs) has driven their success, yet dense Transformers couple capacity and computation: every parameter is activated for every token, making training and inference costs grow linearly with model size-a critical bottleneck as models approach trillion-parameter regimes. We aim to scale capacity through MoE-style mixture throughout the LLM pipeline rather than only the FFN. Prior pipeline-level approaches include ParaScale, which introduces virtual tokens and parallel streams but incurs substantial overhead and suffers from homogenized routing and gradient collapse, and AltUp, which uses an auxiliary prediction branch but offers limited adaptivity and slow convergence. We establish that MoE-style mixture layers can be reformulated as variable-kernel dynamic convolutions, where each expert corresponds to a $1{\times}1$ convolutional kernel and routing implements input-conditioned kernel aggregation.
